## Corpus Check
- 4083 files · ~5,217,437 words

## Summary
- 66615 nodes · 138159 edges · 2975 communities (2402 shown, 222 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7289 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b1d3bd51`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
